It uses a 1D vertical approach with dynamic layer thickness based on density gradients.\n\n## Installation and Setup\n\n### System Requirements\n- GLM is typically installed as the `glm` command-line tool\n- Requires Fortran compiler and NetCDF libraries\n- NetCDF output format support\n\n### Key Concepts\n1. **Namelist Configuration (*.nml)**: Text-based configuration file using Fortran namelist format\n2. **Input Data**: CSV files for meteorology, inflows, and outflows\n3. **Output**: NetCDF format containing simulated water profiles over time\n4. **Key Variables**:\n   - Temperature profiles at multiple depths\n   - Layer thickness and count\n   - Mixing coefficients\n   - Lake morphometry (area-depth relationship)\n\n## Configuration Structure (glm3.nml)\n\n### Main Sections\n\n#### &glm_setup\n- Controls numerical layer resolution\n- `sim_name`: Simulation identifier\n- `max_layers`: Maximum number of water layers (500 typical)\n- `min_layer_vol`: Minimum volume per layer (m³)\n- `min_layer_thick`: Minimum layer thickness (m)\n- `max_layer_thick`: Maximum layer thickness (m)\n- `density_model`: 1 = fresh water density model\n\n#### &time\n- `timefmt`: Format type (2 = formatted string)\n- `start`: Simulation start date/time (YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS)\n- `stop`: Simulation end date/time\n- `dt`: Time step in seconds (3600 = 1 hour)\n- `timezone`: UTC offset (e.g., -6 for Central)\n\n#### &morphometry\n- Lake basin shape definition\n- `lake_name`: Lake identifier\n- `latitude`, `longitude`: Geographic coordinates\n- `crest_elev`: Lake surface elevation (m)\n- `H`: Array of elevation points (m)\n- `A`: Array of corresponding water surface areas (m²)\n- `bsn_len`, `bsn_wid`: Basin length and width (m)\n\n#### &meteorology\n- Atmospheric forcing configuration\n- `met_sw`: Enable meteorology (.true./.false.)\n- `meteo_fl`: Path to meteorological CSV file\n- `wind_factor`, `sw_factor`, `lw_factor`: Scaling factors for inputs\n- Bulk transfer coefficients: `cd`, `ce`, `ch`\n\n#### &inflow\n- Tributary/inflow streams\n- `num_inflows`: Number of inflow streams\n- `names_of_strms`: Stream names array\n- `inflow_fl`: Path to inflow CSV files\n- `inflow_vars`: Variables in inflow files (FLOW, TEMP, SALT, etc.)\n\n#### &outflow\n- Lake outlet configuration\n- `outlet_type`: Outlet mode (1 = fixed elevation)\n- `outl_elvs`: Outlet elevation (m)\n- `outflow_fl`: Path to outflow CSV file\n\n#### &mixing\n- Turbulence and mixing parameters\n- `surface_mixing`: Enable surface mixing\n- `coef_wind_stir`: Wind-driven mixing coefficient\n- `coef_mix_conv`: Convective mixing coefficient\n- `deep_mixing`: Deep mixing mode (2 = shear + convection)\n\n#### &init_profiles\n- Initial temperature and salinity profiles\n- `lake_depth`: Maximum lake depth (m)\n- `num_depths`: Number of depth points\n- `the_depths`: Array of depths (m)\n- `the_temps`: Array of initial temperatures (°C)\n- `the_sals`: Array of initial salinities (g/m³)\n\n#### &output\n- Output file specifications\n- `out_dir`: Output directory\n- `out_fn`: Output filename prefix\n- `nsave`: Save frequency (hours between saves)\n- `csv_point_at`: Depths for CSV point output (m)\n\n## Input Data Format\n\n### Meteorological Data (meteo.csv)\n```\nDate,ShortWave,LongWave,AirTemp,RelHum,WindSpeed,Rain,Snow\n2009-01-01 00:00:00,0,191.51,-15.61,88.87,2.91,0,0\n```\n- ShortWave: Solar radiation (W/m²)\n- LongWave: Longwave radiation (W/m²)\n- AirTemp: Air temperature (°C)\n- RelHum: Relative humidity (%)\n- WindSpeed: Wind speed (m/s)\n- Rain: Precipitation (mm/day)\n- Snow: Snow water equivalent (mm/day)\n\n### Inflow Data (streams, e.g., yahara.csv, pheasant.csv)\n```\ntime,FLOW,SALT,TEMP,...\n2009-01-01 00:00:00,3.56,0,-0.53,...\n```\n- FLOW: Discharge (m³/s)\n- SALT: Salinity (g/m³)\n- TEMP: Temperature (°C)\n- Additional biogeochemical variables as needed\n\n### Outflow Data (outflow.csv)\n```\ntime,FLOW\n2009-01-01 00:00:00,6.09\n```\n\n## Running GLM\n\n### Basic Command\n```bash\nglm glm3.nml\n```\n\n### Typical Workflow\n1. Prepare input CSV files with correct timestamps and variables\n2. Edit glm3.nml with lake-specific parameters\n3. Run GLM: `glm glm3.nml`\n4. Check `output/output.nc` for simulation results\n\n## Output Files\n\n### NetCDF Output (output.nc)\n- Primary output containing full 3D vertical profiles\n- Variables: temperature (temp), layer count (NS), depths\n- Can be read with Python netCDF4 library or ncdump\n\n### CSV Output (optional)\n- Point time series at specified depths\n- Lake average properties\n- Outlet properties\n\n## Common Parameter Tuning for Calibration\n\nWhen RMSE between simulation and observations is too high:\n\n1. **Initial Profiles** (`the_temps`): Adjust initial temperature distribution\n2. **Mixing Coefficients** (`coef_*`): Increase/decrease turbulent mixing intensity\n3. **Wind Factor** (`wind_factor`): Scale wind forcing (typically 0.5-2.0)\n4. **Bulk Transfer Coefficients** (`cd`, `ce`, `ch`): Control air-water momentum/heat exchange\n5. **Time Step** (`dt`): For stability or accuracy (3600s typical for hourly input)\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- **Model crashes**: Check input file timestamps match configuration times\n- **Unrealistic temperatures**: Verify initial profiles and forcing data ranges\n- **Poor validation**: Tune mixing parameters and initial conditions\n- **Missing output**: Verify `out_dir` exists and has write permissions\n\n## References\n- GLM GitHub: https://github.com/AquaticEcoDynamics/GLM\n- Configuration documentation in GLM repository\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":104,"cache_read_input_tokens":24305,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":104,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a771b100-5299-48e2-85e5-0669951f3546","uuid":"c442e792-ef20-4c31-8603-a1dd98c7870b"}

per layer (m³)\n- `min_layer_thick`: Minimum layer thickness (m)\n- `max_layer_thick`: Maximum layer thickness (m)\n- `density_model`: 1 = fresh water density model\n\n#### &time\n- `timefmt`: Format type (2 = formatted string)\n- `start`: Simulation start date/time (YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS)\n- `stop`: Simulation end date/time\n- `dt`: Time step in seconds (3600 = 1 hour)\n- `timezone`: UTC offset (e.g., -6 for Central)\n\n#### &morphometry\n- Lake basin shape definition\n- `lake_name`: Lake identifier\n- `latitude`, `longitude`: Geographic coordinates\n- `crest_elev`: Lake surface elevation (m)\n- `H`: Array of elevation points (m)\n- `A`: Array of corresponding water surface areas (m²)\n- `bsn_len`, `bsn_wid`: Basin length and width (m)\n\n#### &meteorology\n- Atmospheric forcing configuration\n- `met_sw`: Enable meteorology (.true./.false.)\n- `meteo_fl`: Path to meteorological CSV file\n- `wind_factor`, `sw_factor`, `lw_factor`: Scaling factors for inputs\n- Bulk transfer coefficients: `cd`, `ce`, `ch`\n\n#### &inflow\n- Tributary/inflow streams\n- `num_inflows`: Number of inflow streams\n- `names_of_strms`: Stream names array\n- `inflow_fl`: Path to inflow CSV files\n- `inflow_vars`: Variables in inflow files (FLOW, TEMP, SALT, etc.)\n\n#### &outflow\n- Lake outlet configuration\n- `outlet_type`: Outlet mode (1 = fixed elevation)\n- `outl_elvs`: Outlet elevation (m)\n- `outflow_fl`: Path to outflow CSV file\n\n#### &mixing\n- Turbulence and mixing parameters\n- `surface_mixing`: Enable surface mixing\n- `coef_wind_stir`: Wind-driven mixing coefficient\n- `coef_mix_conv`: Convective mixing coefficient\n- `deep_mixing`: Deep mixing mode (2 = shear + convection)\n\n#### &init_profiles\n- Initial temperature and salinity profiles\n- `lake_depth`: Maximum lake depth (m)\n- `num_depths`: Number of depth points\n- `the_depths`: Array of depths (m)\n- `the_temps`: Array of initial temperatures (°C)\n- `the_sals`: Array of initial salinities (g/m³)\n\n#### &output\n- Output file specifications\n- `out_dir`: Output directory\n- `out_fn`: Output filename prefix\n- `nsave`: Save frequency (hours between saves)\n- `csv_point_at`: Depths for CSV point output (m)\n\n## Input Data Format\n\n### Meteorological Data (meteo.csv)\n```\nDate,ShortWave,LongWave,AirTemp,RelHum,WindSpeed,Rain,Snow\n2009-01-01 00:00:00,0,191.51,-15.61,88.87,2.91,0,0\n```\n- ShortWave: Solar radiation (W/m²)\n- LongWave: Longwave radiation (W/m²)\n- AirTemp: Air temperature (°C)\n- RelHum: Relative humidity (%)\n- WindSpeed: Wind speed (m/s)\n- Rain: Precipitation (mm/day)\n- Snow: Snow water equivalent (mm/day)\n\n### Inflow Data (streams, e.g., yahara.csv, pheasant.csv)\n```\ntime,FLOW,SALT,TEMP,...\n2009-01-01 00:00:00,3.56,0,-0.53,...\n```\n- FLOW: Discharge (m³/s)\n- SALT: Salinity (g/m³)\n- TEMP: Temperature (°C)\n- Additional biogeochemical variables as needed\n\n### Outflow Data (outflow.csv)\n```\ntime,FLOW\n2009-01-01 00:00:00,6.09\n```\n\n## Running GLM\n\n### Basic Command\n```bash\nglm glm3.nml\n```\n\n### Typical Workflow\n1. Prepare input CSV files with correct timestamps and variables\n2. Edit glm3.nml with lake-specific parameters\n3. Run GLM: `glm glm3.nml`\n4. Check `output/output.nc` for simulation results\n\n## Output Files\n\n### NetCDF Output (output.nc)\n- Primary output containing full 3D vertical profiles\n- Variables: temperature (temp), layer count (NS), depths\n- Can be read with Python netCDF4 library or ncdump\n\n### CSV Output (optional)\n- Point time series at specified depths\n- Lake average properties\n- Outlet properties\n\n## Common Parameter Tuning for Calibration\n\nWhen RMSE between simulation and observations is too high:\n\n1. **Initial Profiles** (`the_temps`): Adjust initial temperature distribution\n2. **Mixing Coefficients** (`coef_*`): Increase/decrease turbulent mixing intensity\n3. **Wind Factor** (`wind_factor`): Scale wind forcing (typically 0.5-2.0)\n4. **Bulk Transfer Coefficients** (`cd`, `ce`, `ch`): Control air-water momentum/heat exchange\n5. **Time Step** (`dt`): For stability or accuracy (3600s typical for hourly input)\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- **Model crashes**: Check input file timestamps match configuration times\n- **Unrealistic temperatures**: Verify initial profiles and forcing data ranges\n- **Poor validation**: Tune mixing parameters and initial conditions\n- **Missing output**: Verify `out_dir` exists and has write permissions\n\n## References\n- GLM GitHub: https://github.com/AquaticEcoDynamics/GLM\n- Configuration documentation in GLM repository\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

The `netCDF4` library provides Python bindings for reading and writing NetCDF files.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install netCDF4 numpy scipy\n```\n\n## Basic NetCDF4 Usage\n\n### Reading NetCDF Files\n```python\nfrom netCDF4 import Dataset\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Open a NetCDF file\nnc = Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\n\n# List all variables\nprint(nc.variables.keys())\n\n# Get variable information\ntemp = nc.variables['temp']\nprint(f\"Shape: {temp.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Dimensions: {temp.dimensions}\")\n\n# Read data\ntemp_data = temp[:]\n\n# Close file\nnc.close()\n```\n\n### File Structure\n- **Dimensions**: Names and sizes (e.g., time=2000, z=100)\n- **Variables**: Multi-dimensional arrays with attributes\n- **Attributes**: Metadata (units, long_name, etc.)\n- **Groups**: Hierarchical organization (optional)\n\n### Common GLM Output Variables\n- `temp`: Temperature (°C) - typically (time, height)\n- `NS`: Number of layers at each timestep\n- `z`: Layer depths (m)\n- `time`: Simulation time steps\n- `Tz`: Temperature at each layer\n\n## Time Handling\n\n### Converting NetCDF Time\nGLM typically uses days since a reference date. Common approach:\n```python\nfrom netCDF4 import num2date\nimport numpy as np\n\nnc = Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\ntimes = nc.variables['time']\ndates = num2date(times[:], times.units)  # Convert to datetime objects\n\n# Or manual calculation if no time variable\n# GLM time reference: days since 1858-11-17\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nref_date = datetime(1858, 11, 17)\ndays_since = nc.variables['time'][:]\ndates = [ref_date + timedelta(days=d) for d in days_since]\n```\n\n## Extracting Time Series Data\n\n### Getting temperatures at specific depths\n```python\nnc = Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\n\n# Get time dimension\ntime_var = nc.variables['time']\ndates = num2date(time_var[:], time_var.units)\n\n# Get temperature data\ntemp = nc.variables['temp'][:]  # Shape: (time, depth)\n\n# Extract surface temperature (first depth)\nsurface_temp = temp[:, 0]\n\n# Create time series\nimport pandas as pd\nts = pd.DataFrame({\n    'datetime': dates,\n    'temp': surface_temp\n})\n\nnc.close()\n```\n\n## Calculating RMSE\n\n### RMSE Between Observation and Simulation\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error\nimport math\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Root Mean Squared Error\"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = observed[mask]\n    sim_clean = simulated[mask]\n\n    mse = mean_squared_error(obs_clean, sim_clean)\n    rmse = math.sqrt(mse)\n    return rmse\n\n# Example\nobs_temps = np.array([5.0, 5.2, 5.1, 4.9])\nsim_temps = np.array([5.1, 5.0, 5.3, 5.0])\nrmse = calculate_rmse(obs_temps, sim_temps)\nprint(f\"RMSE: {rmse:.3f}°C\")\n```\n\n### Profile RMSE (depth-averaged)\n```python\ndef profile_rmse(obs_profiles, sim_profiles):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE for temperature profiles\"\"\"\n    rmse_list = []\n    for i in range(len(obs_profiles)):\n        rmse = calculate_rmse(obs_profiles[i], sim_profiles[i])\n        rmse_list.append(rmse)\n    return np.mean(rmse_list), np.std(rmse_list)\n```\n\n## Working with Dimensions and Coordinates\n\n### Understanding NetCDF Structure\n```python\nnc = Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\n\n# Print dimensions\nprint(\"Dimensions:\")\nfor name, dim in nc.dimensions.items():\n    print(f\"  {name}: {len(dim)}\")\n\n# Print variables\nprint(\"\\nVariables:\")\nfor name, var in nc.variables.items():\n    print(f\"  {name}: {var.dimensions} {var.shape}\")\n    if hasattr(var, 'units'):\n        print(f\"    units: {var.units}\")\n\n# Access coordinate variables\ntimes = nc.variables['time'][:]\ndepths = nc.variables['z'][:]  # or 'height' or similar\n```\n\n## Comparing Simulated to Observed Data\n\n### Matching Timestamps\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom netCDF4 import Dataset, num2date\n\n# Read observation data\nobs_df = pd.read_csv('field_temp_oxy.csv')\nobs_df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(obs_df['datetime'])\n\n# Read simulation\nnc = Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\ntime_var = nc.variables['time']\nsim_dates = num2date(time_var[:], time_var.units)\nsim_temp = nc.variables['temp'][:]  # Shape: (time, height)\nsim_depths = nc.variables['z'][:]   # Depths at each height\n\n# Find closest simulation times\nobs_dates = obs_df['datetime'].unique()\nfor obs_date in obs_dates:\n    # Find closest simulation timestep\n    time_diff = np.abs(np.array([d - pd.Timestamp(obs_date).to_pydatetime()\n                                  for d in sim_dates]).total_seconds())\n    closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diff)\n\n    # Get simulated profile at that time\n    sim_profile = sim_temp[closest_idx, :]\n\n    # Compare with observations at that date\n    obs_at_date = obs_df[obs_df['datetime'] == obs_date]\n```\n\n## Useful Tips\n\n- **Memory efficiency**: Read specific timesteps instead of full arrays for large files\n- **Attributes**: Store metadata in variable attributes (units, description, etc.)\n- **Compression**: NetCDF can store compressed data for large simulations\n- **Variable naming**: GLM follows CF conventions where possible\n- **Dimension order**: Most NetCDF files use (time, space) ordering\n\n## Common Issues\n\n1. **Time units confusion**: Check `time.units` attribute carefully\n2. **Memory overflow**: Don't load entire dataset if unnecessary\n3. **Missing dimensions**: Some variables may be subsets of dimensions\n4. **Coordinate ordering**: Verify if depths are stored as dimension or variable\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1826,"cache_read_input_tokens":24409,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1826,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a771b100-5299-48e2-85e5-0669951f3546","uuid":"71f1c18d-26c3-4557-8366-512b3e0a5523"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_019C6So56HqaxoLhwghJ9Sha","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01MNUPxwc5KHaBCPW7qUec4q","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/rmse-validation-methods.md","content":"# RMSE Calculation and Model Validation Methods\n\n## Overview\nRoot Mean Squared Error (RMSE) is a fundamental metric for evaluating the accuracy of simulations against observations. For lake temperature modeling, RMSE quantifies how well the model reproduces observed water temperatures.\n\n## RMSE Fundamentals\n\n### Mathematical Definition\n```\nRMSE = √(Σ(yobs - ypred)² / n)\n```\n\nWhere:\n- `yobs`: Observed value\n- `ypred`: Predicted/simulated value\n- `n`: Number of observations\n\n### Python Implementation\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.metrics import mean_squared_error\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, predicted):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE between two arrays\"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(predicted))\n    obs_clean = observed[mask]\n    pred_clean = predicted[mask]\n\n    if len(obs_clean) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    mse = mean_squared_error(obs_clean, pred_clean)\n    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)\n    return rmse\n\n# Example\nobs = np.array([5.0, 5.2, 5.1, 4.9])\npred = np.array([5.1, 5.0, 5.3, 5.0])\nrmse = calculate_rmse(obs, pred)\nprint(f\"RMSE: {rmse:.3f}°C\")  # Output: RMSE: 0.158°C\n```\n\n## Validation for Lake Temperature Profiles\n\n### Profile-Based RMSE\nLake observations typically consist of vertical temperature profiles at discrete times and depths. RMSE calculation requires careful data alignment:\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef profile_rmse(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate RMSE for temperature profiles\n\n    Args:\n        obs_df: DataFrame with columns [datetime, depth, temp]\n        sim_df: DataFrame with columns [datetime, depth, temp]\n\n    Returns:\n        RMSE value in °C\n    \"\"\"\n    # Merge on datetime and depth\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        sim_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        on=['datetime', 'depth'],\n        suffixes=('_obs', '_sim')\n    )\n\n    # Remove NaN values\n    merged = merged.dropna(subset=['temp_obs', 'temp_sim'])\n\n    if len(merged) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim'])**2))\n    return rmse\n```\n\n### Depth-Stratified RMSE\n```python\ndef depth_stratified_rmse(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE separately for each depth\"\"\"\n    rmse_by_depth = {}\n\n    for depth in obs_df['depth'].unique():\n        obs_at_depth = obs_df[obs_df['depth'] == depth]['temp']\n        sim_at_depth = sim_df[sim_df['depth'] == depth]['temp']\n\n        # Align by time\n        merged = pd.DataFrame({\n            'obs': obs_at_depth,\n            'sim': sim_at_depth\n        }).dropna()\n\n        if len(merged) > 0:\n            rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((merged['obs'] - merged['sim'])**2))\n            rmse_by_depth[depth] = rmse\n\n    return rmse_by_depth\n```\n\n### Surface vs. Hypolimnion RMSE\n```python\ndef stratified_validation(obs_df, sim_df, thermocline_depth=10):\n    \"\"\"Separate surface and deep water validation\"\"\"\n    surface_obs = obs_df[obs_df['depth'] <= thermocline_depth]\n    deep_obs = obs_df[obs_df['depth'] > thermocline_depth]\n\n    surface_sim = sim_df[sim_df['depth'] <= thermocline_depth]\n    deep_sim = sim_df[sim_df['depth'] > thermocline_depth]\n\n    surface_rmse = profile_rmse(surface_obs, surface_sim)\n    deep_rmse = profile_rmse(deep_obs, deep_sim)\n\n    return {\n        'surface_rmse': surface_rmse,\n        'deep_rmse': deep_rmse,\n        'overall_rmse': profile_rmse(obs_df, sim_df)\n    }\n```\n\n## Matching Observation and Simulation Timestamps\n\n### Finding Closest Simulation Times\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom datetime import timedelta\n\ndef match_timestamps(obs_times, sim_times, max_diff=timedelta(hours=12)):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match observation times to closest simulation times\n\n    Args:\n        obs_times: Array of observation datetimes\n        sim_times: Array of simulation datetimes\n        max_diff: Maximum allowed time difference\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping obs_time to sim_time\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = {}\n\n    for obs_time in obs_times:\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        time_diff = np.abs(np.array(sim_times) - pd.Timestamp(obs_time))\n        closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diff)\n        sim_time = sim_times[closest_idx]\n\n        # Check if within tolerance\n        if time_diff[closest_idx] <= max_diff:\n            matches[obs_time] = sim_time\n        else:\n            matches[obs_time] = None\n\n    return matches\n```\n\n### Temporal RMSE\n```python\ndef temporal_rmse(obs_df, sim_df, time_col='datetime', depth_col='depth', temp_col='temp'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate RMSE over time periods (seasonal, annual, etc.)\n\n    Args:\n        obs_df, sim_df: DataFrames with datetime index\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with RMSE for each time period\n    \"\"\"\n    # Merge datasets\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df.rename(columns={temp_col: 'temp_obs'}),\n        sim_df.rename(columns={temp_col: 'temp_sim'}),\n        on=[time_col, depth_col],\n        how='inner'\n    )\n\n    # Calculate RMSE by month\n    merged['month'] = pd.to_datetime(merged[time_col]).dt.to_period('M')\n    monthly_rmse = merged.groupby('month').apply(\n        lambda x: np.sqrt(np.mean((x['temp_obs'] - x['temp_sim'])**2))\n    )\n\n    # Calculate RMSE by year\n    merged['year'] = pd.to_datetime(merged[time_col]).dt.year\n    yearly_rmse = merged.groupby('year').apply(\n        lambda x: np.sqrt(np.mean((x['temp_obs'] - x['temp_sim'])**2))\n    )\n\n    return monthly_rmse, yearly_rmse\n```\n\n## Interpretation and Diagnostics\n\n### Performance Thresholds\nFor lake temperature modeling:\n- **RMSE < 1°C**: Excellent simulation\n- **RMSE 1-2°C**: Good simulation (typical target)\n- **RMSE 2-3°C**: Acceptable but needs improvement\n- **RMSE > 3°C**: Poor simulation, needs recalibration\n\n### Diagnostic Plots\n```python\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\ndef plot_validation(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Create diagnostic plots\"\"\"\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        sim_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        on=['datetime', 'depth'],\n        suffixes=('_obs', '_sim')\n    ).dropna()\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12, 10))\n\n    # 1. Time series comparison\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    surface = merged[merged['depth'] == 0]\n    ax.plot(surface['datetime'], surface['temp_obs'], 'o-', label='Observed')\n    ax.plot(surface['datetime'], surface['temp_sim'], 's-', label='Simulated')\n    ax.set_title('Surface Temperature')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # 2. Scatter plot (obs vs. sim)\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    ax.scatter(merged['temp_obs'], merged['temp_sim'], alpha=0.5)\n    ax.plot([merged['temp_obs'].min(), merged['temp_obs'].max()],\n            [merged['temp_obs'].min(), merged['temp_obs'].max()],\n            'k--', label='Perfect fit')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Observed Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Simulated Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.set_title(f\"RMSE: {np.sqrt(np.mean((merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim'])**2)):.2f}°C\")\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # 3. Residuals\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    residuals = merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim']\n    ax.hist(residuals, bins=30, edgecolor='black')\n    ax.axvline(residuals.mean(), color='r', linestyle='--', label=f'Mean: {residuals.mean():.2f}')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Residual (obs - sim) (°C)')\n    ax.set_title('Residual Distribution')\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # 4. RMSE by depth\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    rmse_by_depth = merged.groupby('depth').apply(\n        lambda x: np.sqrt(np.mean((x['temp_obs'] - x['temp_sim'])**2))\n    )\n    ax.plot(rmse_by_depth.values, rmse_by_depth.index, 'o-')\n    ax.set_xlabel('RMSE (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Depth (m)')\n    ax.set_title('RMSE by Depth')\n    ax.invert_yaxis()\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    return fig\n```\n\n## Model Calibration Workflow\n\n### Iterative Improvement\n```python\ndef calibration_workflow(obs_df, glm_output, param_ranges):\n    \"\"\"\n    Test parameter combinations to minimize RMSE\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with parameter sets and corresponding RMSE\n    \"\"\"\n    from itertools import product\n\n    results = []\n\n    for params in product(*param_ranges):\n        # Update GLM config with params\n        # Run GLM\n        # Extract simulation\n        sim_df = extract_temperatures_from_netcdf('output.nc')\n\n        # Calculate RMSE\n        rmse = profile_rmse(obs_df, sim_df)\n\n        results.append({\n            'params': params,\n            'rmse': rmse\n        })\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    return results_df.sort_values('rmse')\n```\n\n## Summary Statistics\n```python\ndef validation_summary(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Generate comprehensive validation report\"\"\"\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        sim_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        on=['datetime', 'depth'],\n        suffixes=('_obs', '_sim')\n    ).dropna()\n\n    residuals = merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim']\n\n    return {\n        'n_samples': len(merged),\n        'rmse': np.sqrt(np.mean(residuals**2)),\n        'mae': np.mean(np.abs(residuals)),\n        'bias': residuals.mean(),\n        'std_residual': residuals.std(),\n        'min_obs': merged['temp_obs'].min(),\n        'max_obs': merged['temp_obs'].max(),\n        'min_sim': merged['temp_sim'].min(),\n        'max_sim': merged['temp_sim'].max(),\n    }\n```\n\n## References\n- Jolliffe, I. T., & Stephenson, D. B. (2011). Forecast verification: a practitioner's guide\n- RMSE interpretation: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Root-mean-square_deviation\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2189,"cache_read_input_tokens":28033,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2189,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a771b100-5299-48e2-85e5-0669951f3546","uuid":"b4ff01db-c586-4cbd-8671-68662828e7fc"}

RMSE calculation requires careful data alignment:\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef profile_rmse(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate RMSE for temperature profiles\n\n    Args:\n        obs_df: DataFrame with columns [datetime, depth, temp]\n        sim_df: DataFrame with columns [datetime, depth, temp]\n\n    Returns:\n        RMSE value in °C\n    \"\"\"\n    # Merge on datetime and depth\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        sim_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        on=['datetime', 'depth'],\n        suffixes=('_obs', '_sim')\n    )\n\n    # Remove NaN values\n    merged = merged.dropna(subset=['temp_obs', 'temp_sim'])\n\n    if len(merged) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim'])**2))\n    return rmse\n```\n\n### Depth-Stratified RMSE\n```python\ndef depth_stratified_rmse(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE separately for each depth\"\"\"\n    rmse_by_depth = {}\n\n    for depth in obs_df['depth'].unique():\n        obs_at_depth = obs_df[obs_df['depth'] == depth]['temp']\n        sim_at_depth = sim_df[sim_df['depth'] == depth]['temp']\n\n        # Align by time\n        merged = pd.DataFrame({\n            'obs': obs_at_depth,\n            'sim': sim_at_depth\n        }).dropna()\n\n        if len(merged) > 0:\n            rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((merged['obs'] - merged['sim'])**2))\n            rmse_by_depth[depth] = rmse\n\n    return rmse_by_depth\n```\n\n### Surface vs. Hypolimnion RMSE\n```python\ndef stratified_validation(obs_df, sim_df, thermocline_depth=10):\n    \"\"\"Separate surface and deep water validation\"\"\"\n    surface_obs = obs_df[obs_df['depth'] <= thermocline_depth]\n    deep_obs = obs_df[obs_df['depth'] > thermocline_depth]\n\n    surface_sim = sim_df[sim_df['depth'] <= thermocline_depth]\n    deep_sim = sim_df[sim_df['depth'] > thermocline_depth]\n\n    surface_rmse = profile_rmse(surface_obs, surface_sim)\n    deep_rmse = profile_rmse(deep_obs, deep_sim)\n\n    return {\n        'surface_rmse': surface_rmse,\n        'deep_rmse': deep_rmse,\n        'overall_rmse': profile_rmse(obs_df, sim_df)\n    }\n```\n\n## Matching Observation and Simulation Timestamps\n\n### Finding Closest Simulation Times\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom datetime import timedelta\n\ndef match_timestamps(obs_times, sim_times, max_diff=timedelta(hours=12)):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match observation times to closest simulation times\n\n    Args:\n        obs_times: Array of observation datetimes\n        sim_times: Array of simulation datetimes\n        max_diff: Maximum allowed time difference\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping obs_time to sim_time\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = {}\n\n    for obs_time in obs_times:\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        time_diff = np.abs(np.array(sim_times) - pd.Timestamp(obs_time))\n        closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diff)\n        sim_time = sim_times[closest_idx]\n\n        # Check if within tolerance\n        if time_diff[closest_idx] <= max_diff:\n            matches[obs_time] = sim_time\n        else:\n            matches[obs_time] = None\n\n    return matches\n```\n\n### Temporal RMSE\n```python\ndef temporal_rmse(obs_df, sim_df, time_col='datetime', depth_col='depth', temp_col='temp'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate RMSE over time periods (seasonal, annual, etc.)\n\n    Args:\n        obs_df, sim_df: DataFrames with datetime index\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with RMSE for each time period\n    \"\"\"\n    # Merge datasets\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df.rename(columns={temp_col: 'temp_obs'}),\n        sim_df.rename(columns={temp_col: 'temp_sim'}),\n        on=[time_col, depth_col],\n        how='inner'\n    )\n\n    # Calculate RMSE by month\n    merged['month'] = pd.to_datetime(merged[time_col]).dt.to_period('M')\n    monthly_rmse = merged.groupby('month').apply(\n        lambda x: np.sqrt(np.mean((x['temp_obs'] - x['temp_sim'])**2))\n    )\n\n    # Calculate RMSE by year\n    merged['year'] = pd.to_datetime(merged[time_col]).dt.year\n    yearly_rmse = merged.groupby('year').apply(\n        lambda x: np.sqrt(np.mean((x['temp_obs'] - x['temp_sim'])**2))\n    )\n\n    return monthly_rmse, yearly_rmse\n```\n\n## Interpretation and Diagnostics\n\n### Performance Thresholds\nFor lake temperature modeling:\n- **RMSE < 1°C**: Excellent simulation\n- **RMSE 1-2°C**: Good simulation (typical target)\n- **RMSE 2-3°C**: Acceptable but needs improvement\n- **RMSE > 3°C**: Poor simulation, needs recalibration\n\n### Diagnostic Plots\n```python\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\ndef plot_validation(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Create diagnostic plots\"\"\"\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        sim_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        on=['datetime', 'depth'],\n        suffixes=('_obs', '_sim')\n    ).dropna()\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12, 10))\n\n    # 1. Time series comparison\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    surface = merged[merged['depth'] == 0]\n    ax.plot(surface['datetime'], surface['temp_obs'], 'o-', label='Observed')\n    ax.plot(surface['datetime'], surface['temp_sim'], 's-', label='Simulated')\n    ax.set_title('Surface Temperature')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # 2. Scatter plot (obs vs. sim)\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    ax.scatter(merged['temp_obs'], merged['temp_sim'], alpha=0.5)\n    ax.plot([merged['temp_obs'].min(), merged['temp_obs'].max()],\n            [merged['temp_obs'].min(), merged['temp_obs'].max()],\n            'k--', label='Perfect fit')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Observed Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Simulated Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.set_title(f\"RMSE: {np.sqrt(np.mean((merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim'])**2)):.2f}°C\")\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # 3. Residuals\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    residuals = merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim']\n    ax.hist(residuals, bins=30, edgecolor='black')\n    ax.axvline(residuals.mean(), color='r', linestyle='--', label=f'Mean: {residuals.mean():.2f}')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Residual (obs - sim) (°C)')\n    ax.set_title('Residual Distribution')\n    ax.legend()\n\n    # 4. RMSE by depth\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    rmse_by_depth = merged.groupby('depth').apply(\n        lambda x: np.sqrt(np.mean((x['temp_obs'] - x['temp_sim'])**2))\n    )\n    ax.plot(rmse_by_depth.values, rmse_by_depth.index, 'o-')\n    ax.set_xlabel('RMSE (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Depth (m)')\n    ax.set_title('RMSE by Depth')\n    ax.invert_yaxis()\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    return fig\n```\n\n## Model Calibration Workflow\n\n### Iterative Improvement\n```python\ndef calibration_workflow(obs_df, glm_output, param_ranges):\n    \"\"\"\n    Test parameter combinations to minimize RMSE\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with parameter sets and corresponding RMSE\n    \"\"\"\n    from itertools import product\n\n    results = []\n\n    for params in product(*param_ranges):\n        # Update GLM config with params\n        # Run GLM\n        # Extract simulation\n        sim_df = extract_temperatures_from_netcdf('output.nc')\n\n        # Calculate RMSE\n        rmse = profile_rmse(obs_df, sim_df)\n\n        results.append({\n            'params': params,\n            'rmse': rmse\n        })\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n    return results_df.sort_values('rmse')\n```\n\n## Summary Statistics\n```python\ndef validation_summary(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Generate comprehensive validation report\"\"\"\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        obs_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        sim_df[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']],\n        on=['datetime', 'depth'],\n        suffixes=('_obs', '_sim')\n    ).dropna()\n\n    residuals = merged['temp_obs'] - merged['temp_sim']\n\n    return {\n        'n_samples': len(merged),\n        'rmse': np.sqrt(np.mean(residuals**2)),\n        'mae': np.mean(np.abs(residuals)),\n        'bias': residuals.mean(),\n        'std_residual': residuals.std(),\n        'min_obs': merged['temp_obs'].min(),\n        'max_obs': merged['temp_obs'].max(),\n        'min_sim': merged['temp_sim'].min(),\n        'max_sim': merged['temp_sim'].max(),\n    }\n```\n\n## References\n- Jolliffe, I. T., & Stephenson, D. B. (2011). Forecast verification: a practitioner's guide\n- RMSE interpretation: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Root-mean-square_deviation\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Load data\n    print(\"\\nLoading observation data...\")\n    obs = load_observations(obs_file)\n\n    print(\"Extracting simulation profiles...\")\n    try:\n        sim = extract_simulation_profiles(nc_file)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error reading simulation file: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n    print(f\"Observation profiles: {len(obs['datetime'].unique())}\")\n    print(f\"Simulation timesteps: {len(sim['datetime'].unique())}\")\n\n    # Match profiles\n    print(\"\\nMatching observation and simulation profiles...\")\n    matched = match_profiles(obs, sim)\n\n    if len(matched) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No matched profiles found!\")\n        return False\n\n    print(f\"Matched samples: {len(matched)}\")\n    print(f\"Matched profiles: {len(matched['datetime'].unique())}\")\n\n    # Calculate overall RMSE\n    overall_rmse = calculate_rmse(matched['temp_obs'].values, matched['temp_sim'].values)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"VALIDATION RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(f\"\\nOverall RMSE: {overall_rmse:.3f}°C\")\n\n    # RMSE by depth\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Depth:\")\n    rmse_by_depth = matched.groupby('obs_depth').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for depth in sorted(rmse_by_depth.index):\n        print(f\"  {depth:5.1f}m: {rmse_by_depth[depth]:6.3f}°C\")\n\n    # RMSE by year\n    matched['year'] = pd.to_datetime(matched['datetime']).dt.year\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Year:\")\n    rmse_by_year = matched.groupby('year').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for year in sorted(rmse_by_year.index):\n        print(f\"  {year}: {rmse_by_year[year]:.3f}°C\")\n\n    # Bias analysis\n    residuals = matched['temp_obs'] - matched['temp_sim']\n    print(f\"\\nResidual Statistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Mean bias: {residuals.mean():.3f}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Std dev: {residuals.std():.3f}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Min: {residuals.min():.3f}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Max: {residuals.max():.3f}°C\")\n\n    # Check target\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    if overall_rmse < 2.0:\n        print(\"✓ VALIDATION SUCCESS: RMSE < 2.0°C\")\n        return True\n    else:\n        print(\"✗ VALIDATION FAILED: RMSE >= 2.0°C\")\n        return False\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    nc_file = '/root/output/output.nc'\n    obs_file = '/root/field_temp_oxy.csv'\n\n    success = validate(nc_file, obs_file)\n    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Get time variable\n    time_var = nc.variables['time']\n    times = num2date(time_var[:], time_var.units)\n\n    # Get temperature data\n    temp_var = nc.variables['temp']\n    temps = temp_var[:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n\n    # Get height/depth variable\n    z_var = nc.variables['z']\n    z_all = z_var[:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n\n    # Get number of layers\n    ns_var = nc.variables['NS']\n    ns = ns_var[:]  # Number of active layers at each timestep\n\n    # Build dataframe\n    profiles = []\n    for t_idx, t in enumerate(times):\n        n_layers = int(ns[t_idx])\n\n        # Extract only valid layers (NS tells us how many)\n        for z_idx in range(n_layers):\n            depth = z_all[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n            temp = temps[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n\n            if not np.isnan(depth) and not np.isnan(temp):\n                profiles.append({\n                    'datetime': t,\n                    'depth': float(depth),\n                    'temp': float(temp)\n                })\n\n    nc.close()\n\n    if len(profiles) == 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"No valid temperature profiles extracted\")\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(profiles)\n\ndef load_observations(csv_file):\n    \"\"\"Load field observations.\"\"\"\n    obs = pd.read_csv(csv_file)\n    obs['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(obs['datetime'])\n    return obs[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']].copy()\n\ndef match_profiles(obs_df, sim_df, max_time_diff=timedelta(hours=12)):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match observation profiles to simulation profiles within tolerance.\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with matched obs and sim temperatures\n    \"\"\"\n    matched = []\n\n    for obs_time in obs_df['datetime'].unique():\n        # Convert to timestamp for comparison\n        obs_timestamp = pd.Timestamp(obs_time)\n\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        sim_times = pd.to_datetime(sim_df['datetime'].unique())\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_times - obs_timestamp)\n        closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n        closest_sim_time = sim_times[closest_idx]\n\n        time_diff = closest_sim_time - obs_timestamp\n        if abs(time_diff) > max_time_diff:\n            continue\n\n        # Get observed profile\n        obs_profile = obs_df[obs_df['datetime'] == obs_time].copy()\n\n        # Get simulated profile at closest time\n        sim_profile = sim_df[sim_df['datetime'] == closest_sim_time].copy()\n\n        if len(obs_profile) == 0 or len(sim_profile) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge on depth (find closest depth in sim for each obs depth)\n        for _, obs_row in obs_profile.iterrows():\n            obs_depth = obs_row['depth']\n\n            # Find closest sim depth\n            depth_diffs = np.abs(sim_profile['depth'] - obs_depth)\n            if len(depth_diffs) > 0:\n                closest_idx = np.argmin(depth_diffs)\n                closest_depth = sim_profile.iloc[closest_idx]['depth']\n\n                if abs(closest_depth - obs_depth) <= 0.5:  # Within 0.5m\n                    matched.append({\n                        'datetime': obs_time,\n                        'obs_depth': obs_depth,\n                        'sim_depth': closest_depth,\n                        'temp_obs': obs_row['temp'],\n                        'temp_sim': sim_profile.iloc[closest_idx]['temp']\n                    })\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(matched)\n\ndef calculate_rmse(obs_temps, sim_temps):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE.\"\"\"\n    diff = obs_temps - sim_temps\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean(diff**2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef validate(nc_file, obs_file):\n    \"\"\"Run full validation.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM SIMULATION VALIDATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Match profiles\n    print(\"\\nMatching observation and simulation profiles...\")\n    matched = match_profiles(obs, sim)\n\n    if len(matched) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No matched profiles found!\")\n        return False\n\n    matched = pd.DataFrame(matched)\n    print(f\"Matched samples: {len(matched)}\")\n    print(f\"Matched profiles: {len(matched['datetime'].unique())}\")\n\n    # Calculate overall RMSE\n    overall_rmse = calculate_rmse(matched['temp_obs'].values, matched['temp_sim'].values)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"VALIDATION RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(f\"\\nOverall RMSE: {overall_rmse:.3f}°C\")\n\n    # RMSE by depth\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Depth:\")\n    rmse_by_depth = matched.groupby('obs_depth').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for depth in sorted(rmse_by_depth.index):\n        print(f\"  {depth:5.1f}m: {rmse_by_depth[depth]:6.3f}°C\")\n\n    # RMSE by year\n    matched['year'] = pd.to_datetime(matched['datetime']).dt.year\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Year:\")\n    rmse_by_year = matched.groupby('year').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for year in sorted(rmse_by_year.index):\n        print(f\"  {year}: {rmse_by_year[year]:.3f}°C\")\n\n    # Bias analysis\n    residuals = matched['temp_obs'] - matched['temp_sim']\n    print(f\"\\nResidual Statistics:\")\n    print(f\"  Mean bias: {residuals.mean():.3f}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Std dev: {residuals.std():.3f}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Min: {residuals.min():.3f}°C\")\n    print(f\"  Max: {residuals.max():.3f}°C\")\n\n    # Check target\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    if overall_rmse < 2.0:\n        print(\"✓ VALIDATION SUCCESS: RMSE < 2.0°C\")\n        return True\n    else:\n        print(\"✗ VALIDATION FAILED: RMSE >= 2.0°C\")\n        return False\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    nc_file = '/root/output/output.nc'\n    obs_file = '/root/field_temp_oxy.csv'\n\n    success = validate(nc_file, obs_file)\n    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1021,"cache_read_input_tokens":40556,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1021,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a771b100-5299-48e2-85e5-0669951f3546","uuid":"057932fb-f207-487e-8801-8b92c1c1a82f"}

Number of active layers at each timestep\n\n    # Build dataframe\n    profiles = []\n    for t_idx, t in enumerate(times):\n        n_layers = int(ns[t_idx])\n\n        # Extract only valid layers (NS tells us how many)\n        for z_idx in range(n_layers):\n            depth = z_all[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n            temp = temps[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n\n            if not np.isnan(depth) and not np.isnan(temp):\n                profiles.append({\n                    'datetime': t,\n                    'depth': float(depth),\n                    'temp': float(temp)\n                })\n\n    nc.close()\n\n    if len(profiles) == 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"No valid temperature profiles extracted\")\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(profiles)\n\ndef load_observations(csv_file):\n    \"\"\"Load field observations.\"\"\"\n    obs = pd.read_csv(csv_file)\n    obs['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(obs['datetime'])\n    return obs[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']].copy()\n\ndef match_profiles(obs_df, sim_df, max_time_diff=timedelta(hours=12)):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match observation profiles to simulation profiles within tolerance.\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with matched obs and sim temperatures\n    \"\"\"\n    matched = []\n\n    for obs_time in obs_df['datetime'].unique():\n        # Convert to timestamp for comparison\n        obs_timestamp = pd.Timestamp(obs_time)\n\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        sim_times = pd.to_datetime(sim_df['datetime'].unique())\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_times - obs_timestamp)\n        closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n        closest_sim_time = sim_times[closest_idx]\n\n        time_diff = closest_sim_time - obs_timestamp\n        if abs(time_diff) > max_time_diff:\n            continue\n\n        # Get observed profile\n        obs_profile = obs_df[obs_df['datetime'] == obs_time].copy()\n\n        # Get simulated profile at closest time\n        sim_profile = sim_df[sim_df['datetime'] == closest_sim_time].copy()\n\n        if len(obs_profile) == 0 or len(sim_profile) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge on depth (find closest depth in sim for each obs depth)\n        for _, obs_row in obs_profile.iterrows():\n            obs_depth = obs_row['depth']\n\n            # Find closest sim depth\n            depth_diffs = np.abs(sim_profile['depth'] - obs_depth)\n            if len(depth_diffs) > 0:\n                closest_idx = np.argmin(depth_diffs)\n                closest_depth = sim_profile.iloc[closest_idx]['depth']\n\n                if abs(closest_depth - obs_depth) <= 0.5:  # Within 0.5m\n                    matched.append({\n                        'datetime': obs_time,\n                        'obs_depth': obs_depth,\n                        'sim_depth': closest_depth,\n                        'temp_obs': obs_row['temp'],\n                        'temp_sim': sim_profile.iloc[closest_idx]['temp']\n                    })\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(matched)\n\ndef calculate_rmse(obs_temps, sim_temps):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE.\"\"\"\n    diff = obs_temps - sim_temps\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean(diff**2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef validate(nc_file, obs_file):\n    \"\"\"Run full validation.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM SIMULATION VALIDATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # RMSE by depth\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Depth:\")\n    rmse_by_depth = matched.groupby('obs_depth').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for depth in sorted(rmse_by_depth.index):\n        print(f\"  {depth:5.1f}m: {rmse_by_depth[depth]:6.3f}°C\")\n\n    # RMSE by year\n    matched['year'] = pd.to_datetime(matched['datetime']).dt.year\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Year:\")\n    rmse_by_year = matched.groupby('year').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for year in sorted(rmse_by_year.index):\n        print(f\"  {year}: {rmse_by_year[year]:.3f}°C\")\n\n    # Check target\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    if overall_rmse < 2.0:\n        print(\"✓ VALIDATION SUCCESS: RMSE < 2.0°C\")\n        return True\n    else:\n        print(\"✗ VALIDATION FAILED: RMSE >= 2.0°C\")\n        return False\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    nc_file = '/root/output/output.nc'\n    obs_file = '/root/field_temp_oxy.csv'\n\n    success = validate(nc_file, obs_file)\n    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":13,"oldLines":6,"newStart":13,"newLines":7,"lines":[" def extract_simulation_profiles(nc_file):","     \"\"\"","     Extract temperature profiles from GLM NetCDF output.","+    Only extracts valid layers based on NS (number of layers)."," ","     Returns:","         DataFrame with columns: datetime, depth, temp"]},{"oldStart":25,"oldLines":33,"newStart":26,"newLines":38,"lines":[" ","     # Get time variable\n    time_var = nc.variables['time']\n    times = num2date(time_var[:], time_var.units)\n\n    # Get temperature data\n    temp_var = nc.variables['temp']\n    temps = temp_var[:]  # Shape: (time, height)\n\n    # Get height/depth dimension\n    if 'z' in nc.variables:\n        heights = nc.variables['z'][:]\n    elif 'height' in nc.variables:\n        heights = nc.variables['height'][:]\n    else:\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_df['datetime'].unique() - pd.Timestamp(obs_time))\n        closest_sim_time = sim_df['datetime'].unique()[np.argmin(time_diffs)]\n\n        time_diff = pd.Timestamp(closest_sim_time) - pd.Timestamp(obs_time)\n        if abs(time_diff) > max_time_diff:\n            continue\n\n        # Get observed profile\n        obs_profile = obs_df[obs_df['datetime'] == obs_time].copy()\n\n        # Get simulated profile at closest time\n        sim_profile = sim_df[sim_df['datetime'] == closest_sim_time].copy()\n\n        if len(obs_profile) == 0 or len(sim_profile) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Get time variable\n    time_var = nc.variables['time']\n    times = num2date(time_var[:], time_var.units)\n\n    # Get temperature data\n    temp_var = nc.variables['temp']\n    temps = temp_var[:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n\n    # Get height/depth variable\n    z_var = nc.variables['z']\n    z_all = z_var[:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n\n    # Get number of layers\n    ns_var = nc.variables['NS']\n    ns = ns_var[:]  # Number of active layers at each timestep\n\n    # Build dataframe\n    profiles = []\n    for t_idx, t in enumerate(times):\n        n_layers = int(ns[t_idx])\n\n        # Extract only valid layers (NS tells us how many)\n        for z_idx in range(n_layers):\n            depth = z_all[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n            temp = temps[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n\n            if not np.isnan(depth) and not np.isnan(temp):\n                profiles.append({\n                    'datetime': t,\n                    'depth': float(depth),\n                    'temp': float(temp)\n                })\n\n    nc.close()\n\n    if len(profiles) == 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"No valid temperature profiles extracted\")\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(profiles)\n\ndef load_observations(csv_file):\n    \"\"\"Load field observations.\"\"\"\n    obs = pd.read_csv(csv_file)\n    obs['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(obs['datetime'])\n    return obs[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']].copy()\n\ndef match_profiles(obs_df, sim_df, max_time_diff=timedelta(hours=12)):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match observation profiles to simulation profiles within tolerance.\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame with matched obs and sim temperatures\n    \"\"\"\n    matched = []\n\n    for obs_time in obs_df['datetime'].unique():\n        # Convert to timestamp for comparison\n        obs_timestamp = pd.Timestamp(obs_time)\n\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        sim_times_raw = sim_df['datetime'].unique()\n        # Convert cftime objects to pandas Timestamp\n        sim_times = np.array([pd.Timestamp(t.isoformat() if hasattr(t, 'isoformat') else str(t))\n                              for t in sim_times_raw])\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_times - obs_timestamp)\n        closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n        closest_sim_time = sim_times[closest_idx]\n\n        time_diff = closest_sim_time - obs_timestamp\n        if abs(time_diff) > max_time_diff:\n            continue\n\n        # Get observed profile\n        obs_profile = obs_df[obs_df['datetime'] == obs_time].copy()\n\n        # Get simulated profile at closest time\n        sim_profile = sim_df[sim_df['datetime'] == closest_sim_time].copy()\n\n        if len(obs_profile) == 0 or len(sim_profile) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge on depth (find closest depth in sim for each obs depth)\n        for _, obs_row in obs_profile.iterrows():\n            obs_depth = obs_row['depth']\n\n            # Find closest sim depth\n            depth_diffs = np.abs(sim_profile['depth'] - obs_depth)\n            if len(depth_diffs) > 0:\n                closest_idx = np.argmin(depth_diffs)\n                closest_depth = sim_profile.iloc[closest_idx]['depth']\n\n                if abs(closest_depth - obs_depth) <= 0.5:  # Within 0.5m\n                    matched.append({\n                        'datetime': obs_time,\n                        'obs_depth': obs_depth,\n                        'sim_depth': closest_depth,\n                        'temp_obs': obs_row['temp'],\n                        'temp_sim': sim_profile.iloc[closest_idx]['temp']\n                    })\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(matched)\n\ndef calculate_rmse(obs_temps, sim_temps):\n    \"\"\"Calculate RMSE.\"\"\"\n    diff = obs_temps - sim_temps\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean(diff**2))\n    return rmse\n\ndef validate(nc_file, obs_file):\n    \"\"\"Run full validation.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM SIMULATION VALIDATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Match profiles\n    print(\"\\nMatching observation and simulation profiles...\")\n    matched = match_profiles(obs, sim)\n\n    if len(matched) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No matched profiles found!\")\n        return False\n\n    matched = pd.DataFrame(matched)\n    print(f\"Matched samples: {len(matched)}\")\n    print(f\"Matched profiles: {len(matched['datetime'].unique())}\")\n\n    # Calculate overall RMSE\n    overall_rmse = calculate_rmse(matched['temp_obs'].values, matched['temp_sim'].values)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"VALIDATION RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(f\"\\nOverall RMSE: {overall_rmse:.3f}°C\")\n\n    # RMSE by depth\n    print(\"\\nRMSE by Depth:\")\n    rmse_by_depth = matched.groupby('obs_depth').apply(\n        lambda x: calculate_rmse(x['temp_obs'].values, x['temp_sim'].values)\n    )\n    for depth in sorted(rmse_by_depth.index):\n        print(f\"  {depth:5.1f}m: {rmse_by_depth[depth]:6.3f}°C\")\n\n    # Build dataframe\n    profiles = []\n    for t_idx, t in enumerate(times):\n        n_layers = int(ns[t_idx])\n\n        for z_idx in range(n_layers):\n            depth = z_all[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n            temp = temps[t_idx, z_idx, 0, 0]\n\n            if not np.isnan(depth) and not np.isnan(temp):\n                profiles.append({\n                    'datetime': pd.Timestamp(t.isoformat() if hasattr(t, 'isoformat') else str(t)),\n                    'depth': float(depth),\n                    'temp': float(temp)\n                })\n\n    nc.close()\n\n    if len(profiles) == 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"No valid temperature profiles extracted\")\n\n    return pd.DataFrame(profiles)\n\ndef load_observations(csv_file):\n    \"\"\"Load field observations.\"\"\"\n    obs = pd.read_csv(csv_file)\n    obs['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(obs['datetime'])\n    return obs[['datetime', 'depth', 'temp']].copy()\n\ndef match_profiles(obs_df, sim_df):\n    \"\"\"Match observation and simulation profiles.\"\"\"\n    matched = []\n\n    for obs_time in obs_df['datetime'].unique():\n        # Find closest simulation time\n        sim_times = pd.to_datetime(sim_df['datetime'].unique())\n        time_diffs = np.abs(sim_times - pd.Timestamp(obs_time))\n        closest_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n        closest_sim_time = sim_times[closest_idx]\n\n        if abs(time_diffs[closest_idx].total_seconds()) > 43200:  # 12 hours\n            continue\n\n        obs_profile = obs_df[obs_df['datetime'] == obs_time].copy()\n        sim_profile = sim_df[sim_df['datetime'] == closest_sim_time].copy()\n\n        if len(obs_profile) == 0 or len(sim_profile) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge on depth\n        for _, obs_row in obs_profile.iterrows():\n            obs_depth = obs_row['depth']\n\n            # Find closest sim depth\n            depth_diffs = np.abs(sim_profile['depth'] - obs_depth)\n            if len(depth_diffs) > 0:\n                closest_d_idx = np.argmin(depth_diffs)\n                closest_depth = sim_profile.iloc[closest_d_idx]['depth']\n\n                if abs(closest_depth - obs_depth) <= 0.5:  # Calculate overall RMSE\n    overall_rmse = calculate_rmse(matched['temp_obs'].values, matched['temp_sim'].values)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"VALIDATION RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(f\"\\n**Overall RMSE: {overall_rmse:.3f}°C**\")\n\n    # discrepancies; calibration for specific depth ranges may be needed if modeling deep-water processes.\n\n3. **Parameter Tuning:** The mixing parameters are conservative to preserve stratification.
